\displaystyle[\![\nu^{-1}K_{\hbar 1}g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}\leq C\varepsilon^{3+\gamma}[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}+C_{\varepsilon}\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}z_{\alpha^{\prime}}w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

for
0\leq\alpha^{\prime}<\frac{1}{2}
,
-3<\gamma\leq 1
,
m\in\mathbb{R}
, sufficiently small
\hbar,\vartheta\geq 0
, and small
\varepsilon>0
to be determined.

Step 2. Control the quantity
[\![\nu^{-1}K_{\hbar 2}^{1-\chi_{\varepsilon}}g]\!]_{m,0,\vartheta}
.

Recalling the definition of
K_{\hbar 2}^{1-\chi_{\varepsilon}}g(x,v)
in (
8.38
), we decompose
K_{\hbar 2}^{1-\chi_{\varepsilon}}g(x,v)=I\!I_{1}+I\!I_{2}
, where

\displaystyle I\!I_{1}=

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}(1-\chi_{\varepsilon})(|v-v_{*}|)e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,v^{\prime})}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})g(x,v^{\prime})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,,

\displaystyle I\!I_{2}=

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}(1-\chi_{\varepsilon})(|v-v_{*}|)e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,v_{*}^{\prime})}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})g(x,v_{*}^{\prime})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

Due to
|v-v_{*}|\leq 2\varepsilon\leq 2
, it follow from (
8.17
) that
\mathfrak{M}(v_{*}^{\prime})\leq C\mathfrak{M}^{1-\delta_{0}}
and
\mathfrak{M}(v_{*}^{\prime})\leq C\mathfrak{M}^{1-\delta_{0}}(v^{\prime})
for
\delta_{0}\in(0,1)
. Together with (
8.39
) (replacing
v_{*}
by
v^{\prime}
), it infers that for sufficiently small
\hbar,\vartheta\geq 0
,

(8.41)

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I_{1}|\leq CI\!I_{1}^{-}\,,

where

\displaystyle I\!I_{1}^{-}=

\displaystyle(1+|v|)^{-\gamma}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}(1+|v-v^{\prime}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1-\delta_{0}}{4}}(v)|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{0,\vartheta}g(x,v^{\prime})|

\displaystyle\times(1-\chi_{\varepsilon})(|v-v_{*}|)e^{(c\hbar+\vartheta)(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1-\delta_{0}}{4}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

For the quantity
I\!I_{1}^{-}
, one easily has

(8.42)

\displaystyle I\!I_{1}^{-}\leq

\displaystyle C[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}(1+|v|)^{-\gamma}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}(1-\chi_{\varepsilon})(|v-v_{*}|)(1+|v-v^{\prime}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1-\delta_{0}}{4}}(v)

\displaystyle\times e^{(c\hbar+\vartheta)(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1-\delta_{0}}{4}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1-\chi_{\varepsilon})(|v-v_{*}|)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1-\delta_{0}}{8}}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\mathrm{d}v_{*}\leq C\varepsilon^{3+\gamma}[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}\,.

Then the bounds (
8.41
) and (
8.42
) indicate that

(8.43)

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I_{1}|\leq C\varepsilon^{3+\gamma}[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}

for
-3<\gamma\leq 1
and sufficiently small
\vartheta,\hbar\geq 0
.

Similarly in (
8.43
), there holds

(8.44)

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I_{2}|\leq C\varepsilon^{3+\gamma}[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}

for
-3<\gamma\leq 1
and sufficiently small
\vartheta,\hbar\geq 0
. Then, by (
8.43
) and (
8.44
), one has

(8.45)

\displaystyle[\![\nu^{-1}K_{\hbar 2}^{1-\chi_{\varepsilon}}g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}\leq C\varepsilon^{3+\gamma}[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}

for
-3<\gamma\leq 1
,
m\in\mathbb{R}
and sufficiently small
\vartheta,\hbar\geq 0
.

Step 3. Control the quantity
[\![\nu^{-1}K_{\hbar 2}^{\chi_{\varepsilon}}g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}
.

Recalling (
8.38
), we split
K_{\hbar 2}^{\chi_{\varepsilon}}g(x,v)=I\!I\!I_{1}+I\!I\!I_{2}
, where

(8.46)

\displaystyle I\!I\!I_{1}=

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\chi_{\varepsilon}(|v-v_{*}|)e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,v^{\prime})}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})g(x,v^{\prime})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,,

\displaystyle I\!I\!I_{2}=

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\chi_{\varepsilon}(|v-v_{*}|)e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,v_{*}^{\prime})}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})g(x,v_{*}^{\prime})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

Step 3.1. Estimate of
[\![\nu^{-1}I\!I\!I_{1}]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}
.
We first divide the quantity
I\!I\!I_{1}
into two parts:

(8.47)

\displaystyle I\!I\!I_{1}=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|v^{\prime}-v|\leq\tilde{\varepsilon}|v_{*}-v|\}}(\cdots)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}}_{:=I\!I\!I_{11}}+\underbrace{\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|v^{\prime}-v|>\tilde{\varepsilon}|v_{*}-v|\}}(v^{\prime})(\cdots)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}}_{:=I\!I\!I_{12}}\,,

where
\tilde{\varepsilon}>0
is small enough to be determined.

Step 3.1.1. Estimate the quantity
|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{11}|
. If
|v^{\prime}-v|\leq\tilde{\varepsilon}|v_{*}-v|
, one has

\displaystyle b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\leq C|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\tfrac{|v^{\prime}-v|}{|v_{*}-v|}\leq C\tilde{\varepsilon}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\,.

Together with (
8.39
) (replacing
v_{*}
by
v^{\prime}
), it is easy to derive that

(8.48)

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{11}|\leq C\tilde{\varepsilon}\widetilde{I\!I\!I}_{11}[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}\,,

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{I\!I\!I}_{11}=\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\tfrac{\chi_{\varepsilon}(|v_{*}-v|)}{|v_{*}-v|^{-\gamma}}(1+|v|)^{-\gamma}(1+|v^{\prime}-v|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{(c\hbar+\vartheta)\big||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}\big|}e^{-\tfrac{1}{4T}(|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|v_{*}^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})}\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

Note that
\tfrac{\chi_{\varepsilon}(|v_{*}-v|)}{|v_{*}-v|^{-\gamma}}(1+|v|)^{-\gamma}\leq C_{\varepsilon}(1+|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|)^{|\gamma|}
, which means that
\tfrac{\chi_{\varepsilon}(|v_{*}-v|)}{|v_{*}-v|^{-\gamma}(1+|v|)^{\gamma}}e^{-\tfrac{|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{8T}}\leq C_{\varepsilon}
. Then

(8.49)

\displaystyle\widetilde{I\!I\!I}_{11}\leq C_{\varepsilon}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}(1+|v^{\prime}-v|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{(c\hbar+\vartheta)\big||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}\big|}e^{-\tfrac{1}{8T}(|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|v_{*}^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})}\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

By letting
V=v_{*}-v
,
V_{\shortparallel}=(V\cdot\omega)\omega
,
V_{\perp}=V-V_{\shortparallel}
and
\omega=\frac{v^{\prime}-v}{|v^{\prime}-v|}
, one has
\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}=2|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-2}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}
, see Section 2 of
[
12
]
. Moreover,
v^{\prime}=v+V_{\shortparallel}
,
v_{*}^{\prime}=v+V_{\perp}
,
v_{*}=v+V
. Recall (
8.20
), hence,
\zeta=v+\tfrac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}\,,\zeta_{\shortparallel}=[(\zeta-\mathfrak{u})\cdot\omega]\omega\,,\zeta_{\perp}=(\zeta-\mathfrak{u})-\zeta_{\shortparallel}
. Combining with (
8.21
) and (
8.24
), one has

\displaystyle\widetilde{I\!I\!I}_{11}\leq

\displaystyle 2C_{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-2}(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{-\tfrac{1}{4T}(|\zeta_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+\tfrac{1}{4}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2})+2(c\hbar+\vartheta)|V_{\shortparallel}\cdot\zeta_{\shortparallel}|}e^{-\tfrac{|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}{4T}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\leq C_{\varepsilon}

provided that
\hbar,\vartheta\geq 0
are both sufficiently small. It therefor follows that

(8.50)

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{11}|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\tilde{\varepsilon}[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}\,.

Step 3.1.2. Estimate the quantity
|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{12}|
. It easily follows from (
8.11
) that
\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)\sigma_{x}^{-\frac{m}{2}}(x,v^{\prime})\leq C(1+|v-v^{\prime}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}
. Then by Lemma
2.1
,

\displaystyle|I\!I\!I_{12}|\leq C\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\chi_{\varepsilon}(|v_{*}-v|)e^{c\hbar||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v^{\prime})|g(x,v^{\prime})|

\displaystyle\times(1+|v-v^{\prime}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|v^{\prime}-v|>\tilde{\varepsilon}|v_{*}-v|\}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\tfrac{|v^{\prime}-v|}{|v_{*}-v|}\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

Note that
w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)\leq C(1+|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|)^{|\gamma|}e^{2\vartheta|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}\cdot(1+|v_{*}-v|)^{|\gamma|}e^{2\vartheta|v_{*}-v|^{2}}
, which means that

\displaystyle w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})\leq C(1+|v_{*}-v|)^{|\gamma|}e^{2\vartheta|v_{*}-v|^{2}}

for sufficiently small
\vartheta\geq 0
. Moreover, one has
\tfrac{\chi_{\varepsilon}}{|v_{*}-v|^{1-\gamma}}\leq C_{\varepsilon}(1+|v_{*}-v|)^{\gamma-1}
. Then

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{12}|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v^{\prime}-v|(1+|v-v^{\prime}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}(1+|v_{*}-v|)^{|\gamma|+\gamma-1}e^{2\vartheta|v_{*}-v|^{2}}

\displaystyle\times e^{c\hbar||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v^{\prime})(\delta x+l)^{\mathfrak{n}_{\gamma}}|g(x,v^{\prime})|e^{-\tfrac{1}{8T}(|v_{*}^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})}\mathbf{1}_{\{|v^{\prime}-v|>\tilde{\varepsilon}|v_{*}-v|\}}\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

By employing the change variables
(\omega,v_{*})\mapsto(V_{\perp},V_{\shortparallel})
, one gains

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{12}|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}(1+|V|)^{|\gamma|+\gamma-1}e^{2\vartheta|V|^{2}}

\displaystyle\times e^{2c\hbar|V_{\shortparallel}\cdot\zeta_{\shortparallel}|}|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}g(x,v+V_{\shortparallel})|e^{-\tfrac{\frac{1}{4}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|\zeta_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}{4T}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|V_{\shortparallel}|>\tilde{\varepsilon}|V|\}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}V_{\shortparallel}\,.

Observe that for sufficiently small
\hbar\geq 0

\displaystyle e^{-\tfrac{\frac{1}{4}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|\zeta_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}{4T}+2c\hbar|V_{\shortparallel}\cdot\zeta_{\shortparallel}|}\mathbf{1}_{\{|V_{\shortparallel}|>\tilde{\varepsilon}|V|\}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle e^{-C_{0}(|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|\zeta_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2})}\mathbf{1}_{\{|V_{\shortparallel}|>\tilde{\varepsilon}|V|\}}\leq e^{-\frac{C_{0}\tilde{\varepsilon}^{2}}{2}|V|^{2}}e^{-\frac{C_{0}}{2}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}e^{-C_{0}|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\,,

and
(1+|V|)^{|\gamma|+\gamma-1}e^{-\frac{C_{0}\tilde{\varepsilon}^{2}}{2}|V|^{2}}\leq C_{\tilde{\varepsilon}}
,
\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}e^{-C_{0}|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\leq C
. Then one has

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{12}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{\varepsilon,\tilde{\varepsilon}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{-\frac{C_{0}}{2}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}g(x,v+V_{\shortparallel})|\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\varepsilon,\tilde{\varepsilon}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{\alpha^{\prime}}^{2}(v+V_{\shortparallel})|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}g(x,v+V_{\shortparallel})|^{2}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-2}(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|)^{|m|(1-\gamma)}z_{-\alpha^{\prime}}^{2}(v+V_{\shortparallel})e^{-C_{0}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

Due to
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-2}(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|)^{|m|(1-\gamma)}z_{-\alpha^{\prime}}^{2}(v+V_{\shortparallel})e^{-C_{0}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\leq C
uniformly in
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
whence
0\leq\alpha^{\prime}<\frac{1}{2}
. It thereby infers that for
0\leq\alpha^{\prime}<\frac{1}{2}
,

(8.51)

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{12}|\leq C_{\varepsilon,\tilde{\varepsilon}}\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}z_{\alpha^{\prime}}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,.

Then (
8.50
) and (
8.51
) imply that

(8.52)

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{1}|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\tilde{\varepsilon}[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}+C_{\varepsilon,\tilde{\varepsilon}}\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}z_{\alpha^{\prime}}w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

for
0\leq\alpha^{\prime}<\frac{1}{2}
,
-3<\gamma\leq 1
,
m\in\mathbb{R}
and sufficiently small
\hbar,\vartheta\geq 0
, where
\varepsilon,\tilde{\varepsilon}>0
are both small to be determined later.

Step 3.2. Estimate of
[\![\nu^{-1}I\!I\!I_{2}]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}
.
Recalling the definition of
I\!I\!I_{2}
in (
8.46
), we decompose it as

\displaystyle I\!I\!I_{2}=I\!I\!I_{21}+I\!I\!I_{22}\,,

where

\displaystyle I\!I\!I_{21}=\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\chi_{\varepsilon}(|v-v_{*}|)e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,v_{*}^{\prime})}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})g(x,v_{*}^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{\{|v_{*}^{\prime}-v|\leq\tilde{\varepsilon}|v_{*}-v|\}}b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,,

and

\displaystyle I\!I\!I_{22}=\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\chi_{\varepsilon}(|v-v_{*}|)e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,v_{*}^{\prime})}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})g(x,v_{*}^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{\{|v_{*}^{\prime}-v|>\tilde{\varepsilon}|v_{*}-v|\}}b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

Step 3.2.1. Control the quantity
|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{21}|
. By
b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\leq C|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\frac{|v^{\prime}-v|}{|v_{*}-v|}\leq C|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}
and
\frac{\chi_{\varepsilon}(|v_{*}-v|)(1+|v|)^{-\gamma}}{|v_{*}-v|^{-\gamma}}\leq C_{\varepsilon}(1+|v_{*}|)^{|\gamma|}
, one has

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{21}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{\varepsilon}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\tfrac{e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{0,\vartheta}(v)}{e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v_{*}^{\prime})}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v_{*}^{\prime})w_{0,\vartheta}(v_{*}^{\prime})}(1+|v_{*}|)^{|\gamma|}\mathbf{1}_{\{|v_{*}^{\prime}-v|\leq\tilde{\varepsilon}|v_{*}-v|\}}

\displaystyle\times|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v_{*}^{\prime})w_{0,\vartheta}(v_{*}^{\prime})g(x,v_{*}^{\prime})|\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\varepsilon}[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}\widetilde{I\!I\!I}_{21}\,,

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{I\!I\!I}_{21}=\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}(1+|v_{*}^{\prime}-v|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{(c\hbar+\vartheta)||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v_{*}^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}e^{-\frac{|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{8T}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|v_{*}^{\prime}-v|\leq\tilde{\varepsilon}|v_{*}-v|\}}\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

Here we have used the bound
(1+|v_{*}|)^{|\gamma|}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})\leq Ce^{-\frac{|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{8T}}
and the inequality (
8.39
) (replacing
v_{*}
by
v_{*}^{\prime}
). By employing the change variables
(\omega,v_{*})\mapsto(V_{\shortparallel},V_{\perp})
and combining with the relations (
8.28
)-(
8.30
), it holds that for sufficiently small
\hbar,\vartheta\geq 0

\displaystyle\widetilde{I\!I\!I}_{21}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}(1+|V_{\perp}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{(c\hbar+\vartheta)|-|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}|}\mathbf{1}_{\{|V_{\perp}|\leq\tilde{\varepsilon}|V|\}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-\tfrac{|\zeta_{\shortparallel}+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}{4T}-\frac{|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}{8T}-\frac{|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}{8T}}\cdot 2|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-2}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}(1+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}(1+|\zeta_{\perp}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-2}

\displaystyle\times e^{-C_{1}(|\zeta_{\shortparallel}+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2})}\mathbf{1}_{\{|V_{\perp}|\leq\frac{\tilde{\varepsilon}}{\sqrt{1-\tilde{\varepsilon}^{2}}}|V_{\shortparallel}|\}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-2}e^{-C_{1}|\zeta_{\shortparallel}+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|V_{\perp}|\leq\frac{\tilde{\varepsilon}}{\sqrt{1-\tilde{\varepsilon}^{2}}}|V_{\shortparallel}|\}}e^{-\frac{C_{1}}{2}|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\,.

Thanks to
\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}e^{-\frac{C_{1}}{2}|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\leq C<+\infty
uniformly in
V_{\shortparallel}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we know that for any
\hat{\varepsilon}>0
there is a
\tilde{\varepsilon}_{\hat{\varepsilon}}>0
such that for any
0<\tilde{\varepsilon}<\tilde{\varepsilon}_{\hat{\varepsilon}}
,
\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|V_{\perp}|\leq\frac{\tilde{\varepsilon}}{\sqrt{1-\tilde{\varepsilon}^{2}}}|V_{\shortparallel}|\}}e^{-\frac{C_{1}}{2}|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\leq\hat{\varepsilon}
. Then the quantity
\widetilde{I\!I\!I}_{21}
can be bounded by
\widetilde{I\!I\!I}_{21}\leq C\hat{\varepsilon}
, which means that

(8.53)

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{21}|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\hat{\varepsilon}[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}

for any
0<\tilde{\varepsilon}<\tilde{\varepsilon}_{\hat{\varepsilon}}
.

Step 3.2.2. Control the quantity
|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{22}|
. Observe that

\displaystyle w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})\leq C(1+|v_{*}-v|)^{|\gamma|}e^{2\vartheta|v_{*}-v|^{2}}e^{-\tfrac{1}{8T}(|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})}\,,

\displaystyle\chi_{\varepsilon}(|v_{*}-v|)b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\leq C_{\varepsilon}|v^{\prime}-v|(1+|v_{*}-v|)^{\gamma-1}\,,

\displaystyle\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)\sigma_{x}^{-\frac{m}{2}}(x,v_{*}^{\prime})e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,v_{*}^{\prime})}\leq C(1+|v-v_{*}^{\prime}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{c\hbar||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v_{*}^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}\,.

Then

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{22}|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v^{\prime}-v|(1+|v-v_{*}^{\prime}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}(1+|v_{*}-v|)^{|\gamma|+\gamma-1}e^{2\vartheta|v_{*}-v|^{2}}

\displaystyle\times e^{c\hbar||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v_{*}^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}g(x,v_{*}^{\prime})|e^{-\tfrac{1}{8T}(|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})}\mathbf{1}_{|v_{*}^{\prime}-v|>\tilde{\varepsilon}|v_{*}-v|}\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

By employing the change variables
(\omega,v_{*})\mapsto(V_{\shortparallel},V_{\perp})
and the similar computations of
\widetilde{I\!I\!I}_{21}
, one has

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{22}|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}e^{-C_{2}|\zeta_{\shortparallel}+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}(1+|V|)^{|\gamma|+\gamma-1}e^{2\vartheta|V|^{2}}

\displaystyle\times|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}g(x,v+V_{\perp})|\mathbf{1}_{\{|V_{\perp}|>\tilde{\varepsilon}|V|\}}(1+|V_{\perp}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{-C_{2}(|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2})}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\,.

If
|V_{\perp}|>\tilde{\varepsilon}|V|
, there holds
|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}\geq|V_{\perp}|^{2}>\tilde{\varepsilon}^{2}|V|^{2}
. Together with
(1+|V_{\perp}|)^{\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{-\frac{C_{2}}{4}(|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2})}\leq C
and
(1+|V|)^{|\gamma|+\gamma-1}e^{-(\frac{C_{2}}{2}\tilde{\varepsilon}^{2}-2\vartheta)|V|^{2}}\leq C_{\tilde{\varepsilon}}
under
0\leq\vartheta<\frac{C_{2}}{4}\tilde{\varepsilon}^{2}
, one has

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{22}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{\varepsilon,\tilde{\varepsilon}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}e^{-C_{2}|\zeta_{\shortparallel}+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}g(x,v+V_{\perp})|e^{-\frac{C_{2}}{4}|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{\varepsilon,\tilde{\varepsilon}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}g(x,v+V_{\perp})|e^{-\frac{C_{2}}{4}|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}e^{-C_{2}|\zeta_{\shortparallel}+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\varepsilon,\tilde{\varepsilon}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}g(x,v+V_{\perp})|e^{-\frac{C_{2}}{4}|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\,.

Since
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha^{\prime}}^{2}(v+V_{\perp})e^{-\frac{C_{2}}{2}|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\leq C
uniformly in
v,\zeta_{\perp}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
whence
0\leq\alpha^{\prime}<\frac{1}{2}
, we obtain

(8.54)

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{22}|\leq C_{\varepsilon,\tilde{\varepsilon}}\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}z_{\alpha^{\prime}}w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,.

Then (
8.53
) and (
8.54
) indicate that for any
\hat{\varepsilon}>0
there is an
\tilde{\varepsilon}_{\hat{\varepsilon}}>0
such that for all
\tilde{\varepsilon}\in(0,\tilde{\varepsilon}_{\hat{\varepsilon}})
,

(8.55)

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)I\!I\!I_{2}|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\hat{\varepsilon}[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}+C_{\varepsilon,\tilde{\varepsilon}}\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}z_{\alpha^{\prime}}w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,,

provided that
\hbar,\vartheta\geq 0
are both small enough. It therefore follows from (
8.52
) and (
8.55
) that for any fixed
\varepsilon,\hat{\varepsilon}>0
,
-3<\gamma\leq 1
,
m\in\mathbb{R}
and
0\leq\alpha^{\prime}<\frac{1}{2}

(8.56)

\displaystyle[\![\nu^{-1}K_{\hbar 2}^{\chi_{\varepsilon}}g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}\leq C_{\varepsilon}(\tilde{\varepsilon}+\hat{\varepsilon})[\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}+C_{\varepsilon,\tilde{\varepsilon}}\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}z_{\alpha^{\prime}}w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

for all
\tilde{\varepsilon}\in(0,\tilde{\varepsilon}_{\hat{\varepsilon}})
, provided that
\hbar,\vartheta\geq 0
are both small enough. Consequently, (
8.40
), (
8.45
) and (
8.56
) show that

\displaystyle[\![\nu^{-1}K_{\hbar}g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}\leq[C\varepsilon^{3+\gamma}+C_{\varepsilon}(\hat{\varepsilon}+\tilde{\varepsilon})][\![g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}+C_{\varepsilon,\tilde{\varepsilon}}\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}z_{\alpha^{\prime}}w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,.

For any fixed
\eta_{1}>0
, we first take
\varepsilon>0
such that
C\varepsilon^{3+\gamma}\leq\frac{\eta_{1}}{3}
and fixed. Then we take
\hat{\varepsilon}>0
such that
C_{\varepsilon}\hat{\varepsilon}\leq\frac{\eta_{1}}{3}
and fixed. At the end, we choose
\tilde{\varepsilon}\in(0,\tilde{\varepsilon}_{\hat{\varepsilon}})
such that
C_{\varepsilon}\tilde{\varepsilon}\leq\frac{\eta_{1}}{3}
and fixed. Therefore, the above inequality finish the proof of Lemma
2.4
.
∎

8.3.
Weighted
L^{2}_{x,v}
property of
K
: Proof of Lemma
2.5

In this section,

Proof of Lemma
2.5
.

By (
1.8
)-(
1.9
)-(
1.10
), one knows that

\displaystyle K_{\hbar}g=-K_{\hbar 1}g+K_{\hbar 2}g+K_{\hbar 3}g\,,

where

(8.57)

\displaystyle K_{\hbar 1}g(x,v)

\displaystyle=e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\hbar\sigma(x,v_{*})}g(x,v_{*})|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,,

(8.58)

\displaystyle K_{\hbar 2}g(x,v)

\displaystyle=e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}e^{-\hbar\sigma(x,v^{\prime})}g(x,v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,,

(8.59)

\displaystyle K_{\hbar 3}g(x,v)

\displaystyle=e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}e^{-\hbar\sigma(x,v_{*}^{\prime})}g(x,v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

Step 1. Estimates for
K_{\hbar 1}
part.

Claim that for
0\leq\alpha<\min\{\frac{1}{2},\frac{\gamma+3}{2}\}
,

(8.60)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 1}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

Indeed, we rewrite

\displaystyle\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 1}g(x,v)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathfrak{k}_{\hbar 1}(x,v,v_{*})\hat{g}(x,v_{*})\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,,

where
\hat{g}:=\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g
, and

\displaystyle\mathfrak{k}_{\hbar 1}(x,v,v_{*})=z_{-\alpha}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})\tfrac{w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v)\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)}}{w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v_{*})\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v_{*})e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v_{*})}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})\,.

By (
8.12
), one has

\displaystyle\tfrac{w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v)\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)}}{w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v_{*})\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v_{*})e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v_{*})}}\lesssim(1+|v-v_{*}|)^{|\beta|+\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{(c\hbar+\vartheta)||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}\,.

Then for sufficiently small
\hbar,\vartheta\geq 0
,
\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})\tfrac{w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v)\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)}}{w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v_{*})\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v_{*})e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v_{*})}}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})\lesssim 1
uniformly in
x
,
v
and
v_{*}
, which yields that
|\mathfrak{k}_{\hbar 1}(x,v,v_{*})|\lesssim z_{-\alpha}(v)|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})
uniformly in
x
. Then

\displaystyle I:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 1}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathfrak{k}_{\hbar 1}(x,v,v_{*})\hat{g}(x,v_{*})\mathrm{d}v_{*}|^{2}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}(v)|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})|\hat{g}(x,v_{*})|\mathrm{d}v_{*}\Big)^{2}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})\mathrm{d}v_{*}\Big)\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})|\hat{g}(x,v_{*})|^{2}\mathrm{d}v_{*}\Big)\mathrm{d}v\,.

Note that by Lemma 2.3 of
[
19
]
,
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})\mathrm{d}v_{*}\lesssim(1+|v|)^{\gamma}
, which means that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})\mathrm{d}v_{*}\lesssim z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v)(1+|v|)^{\gamma}\lesssim z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{8}}(v)\,.

It thereby holds

\displaystyle I\lesssim\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{3}{8}}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}|\hat{g}(x,v_{*})|^{2}\mathrm{d}v_{*}\mathrm{d}v=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})|\hat{g}(x,v_{*})|^{2}\mathsf{h}(v_{*})\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,,

where

\displaystyle\mathsf{h}(v_{*})=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{3}{8}}(v)\mathrm{d}v\,.

One asserts that

(8.61)

\displaystyle\mathsf{h}(v_{*})\lesssim(1+|v_{*}|)^{\gamma}

for
-3<\gamma\leq 1
and
0\leq\alpha<\min\{\frac{1}{2},\frac{\gamma+3}{3}\}
. Once the assertion (
8.61
) holds, one has

\displaystyle I\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1+|v_{*}|)^{\gamma}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})|\hat{g}(x,v_{*})|^{2}\mathrm{d}v_{*}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\hat{g}(x,v_{*})|^{2}\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,,

which means the claim (
8.60
) holds.

It remains to show the assertion (
8.61
). Note that

\displaystyle\mathsf{h}(v_{*})=\underbrace{\int_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}(\cdots)\mathrm{d}v}_{:=\mathsf{h}_{1}(v_{*})}+\underbrace{\int_{|v-v_{*}|>1}(\cdots)\mathrm{d}v}_{:=\mathsf{h}_{2}(v_{*})}\,.

Recalling the definition of
z_{-\alpha}
in (
1.29
), one has

\displaystyle\mathsf{h}_{1}(v_{*})\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1,|v_{3}|\leq 1}|v_{3}|^{-2\alpha}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\mathrm{d}v+\int_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1,|v_{3}|>1}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}|v_{3}|^{-2\alpha}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\mathrm{d}v+\int_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\mathrm{d}v\,.

By letting
v_{1}-v_{*1}=r\sin\varphi\cos\theta\,,\ v_{2}-v_{*2}=r\sin\varphi\sin\theta\,,\ v_{3}-v_{*3}=r\cos\varphi
with
0\leq r\leq 1
,
0\leq\theta\leq 2\pi
and
0\leq\varphi\leq\pi
, a direct computation shows

\displaystyle\mathsf{h}_{1}(v_{*})\lesssim 2\pi\int_{0}^{1}r^{2+\gamma-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\big(|\cos\varphi+\tfrac{|v_{*3}|}{r}|^{-2\alpha}+|\cos\varphi-\tfrac{|v_{*3}|}{r}|^{-2\alpha}\big)\sin\varphi\mathrm{d}\varphi\mathrm{d}r+\tfrac{4\pi}{3+\gamma}\,.

Observe that for
0\leq\alpha<\frac{1}{2}
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\big(|\cos\varphi+\tfrac{|v_{*3}|}{r}|^{-2\alpha}+|\cos\varphi-\tfrac{|v_{*3}|}{r}|^{-2\alpha}\big)\sin\varphi\mathrm{d}\varphi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} \tfrac{1}{1-2\alpha}\big[(\tfrac{|v_{*3}|}{r}+1)^{1-2\alpha}-(\tfrac{|v_{*3}|}{r}-1)^{1-2\alpha}\big]\,,&\quad\tfrac{|v_{*3}|}{r}\geq 1\\
\tfrac{1}{1-2\alpha}\big[(1+\tfrac{|v_{*3}|}{r})^{1-2\alpha}-(1-\tfrac{|v_{*3}|}{r})^{1-2\alpha}\big]\,,&\quad 0\leq\tfrac{|v_{*3}|}{r}<1\end{aligned}\right.\quad\leq C_{\alpha}<\infty

uniformly in
\tfrac{|v_{*3}|}{r}\geq 0
, which implies that

(8.62)

\displaystyle\mathsf{h}_{1}(v_{*})\lesssim 2\pi C_{\alpha}\int_{0}^{1}r^{2+\gamma-2\alpha}\mathrm{d}r+\tfrac{4\pi}{3+\gamma}=\tfrac{2\pi C_{\alpha}}{3+\gamma-2\alpha}+\tfrac{4\pi}{3+\gamma}<\infty\,,

provided that
0\leq\alpha<\min\{\frac{1}{2},\frac{3+\gamma}{2}\}
.

For the case
|v-v_{*}|>1
, one can assert that
|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\lesssim(1+|v|)^{|\gamma|}(1+|v_{*}|)^{\gamma}
for
-3<\gamma\leq 1
. Indeed, if
0\leq\gamma\leq 1
, the bound
|v-v_{*}|\leq(1+|v|)(1+|v_{*}|)
implies the assertion for the case
0\leq\gamma\leq 1
. If
-3<\gamma<0
, one has
1+|v_{*}|\leq(1+|v|)(1+|v-v_{*}|)\leq 2(1+|v|)|v-v_{*}|
, hence,
|v-v_{*}|^{-1}\leq 2(1+|v|)(1+|v_{*}|)^{-1}
, which infers the assertion for the case
-3<\gamma<0
. Then it follows

\displaystyle\mathsf{h}_{2}(v_{*})\lesssim\int_{|v-v_{*}|>1}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)(1+|v|)^{|\gamma|}(1+|v_{*}|)^{\gamma}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{3}{8}}(v)\mathrm{d}v\lesssim(1+|v_{*}|)^{\gamma}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v)\mathrm{d}v\,.

Thanks to
0\leq\alpha<\frac{1}{2}
, one has
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v)\mathrm{d}v\lesssim 1
, which implies

(8.63)

\displaystyle\mathsf{h}_{2}(v_{*})\lesssim(1+|v_{*}|)^{\gamma}\,.

Then the bounds (
8.62
) and (
8.63
) conclude the assertion (
8.61
).

Step 2. Estimates for
K_{\hbar 2}
part.

One asserts that for
0\leq\alpha<\min\{\frac{1}{2},\frac{b_{0}+\gamma+1}{2}\}
with
b_{0}\in\mathcal{S}_{\gamma}
given in Lemma
2.5
,

(8.64)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 2}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

Indeed, let
\chi(r)
be a smooth monotone function satisfying
\chi(r)=0
for
0\leq r\leq 1
and
\chi(r)=1
for
r\geq 2
. Then the operator in (
8.58
) can be decomposed as follows:

(8.65)

\displaystyle K_{\hbar 2}g(x,v)=K_{\hbar 2}^{\chi}g(x,v)+K_{\hbar 2}^{1-\chi}g(x,v)\,,

where for
a=\chi
or
1-\chi

\displaystyle K_{\hbar 2}^{a}g(x,v)=e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}e^{-\hbar\sigma(x,v^{\prime})}a(|v-v_{*}|)g(x,v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

Step 2.1. Control of
K_{\hbar 2}^{\chi}
.

Denote by

\displaystyle\widetilde{K}_{\hbar 2}^{\chi}g(x,v)=z_{-\alpha}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v)K_{\hbar 2}^{\chi}g(x,v)\,.

By (
8.12
),

(8.66)

\displaystyle\tfrac{w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v)\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)}}{w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v^{\prime})\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v^{\prime})e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v^{\prime})}}\lesssim(1+|v-v^{\prime}|)^{|\beta|+\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{(c\hbar+\vartheta)||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}\,.

Then

\displaystyle|\widetilde{K}_{\hbar 2}^{\chi}g(x,v)|\lesssim z_{-\alpha}(v)\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}(1+|v-v^{\prime}|)^{|\beta|+\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{(c\hbar+\vartheta)||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})

\displaystyle\times|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}g(x,v^{\prime})|\chi(|v-v_{*}|)b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,,

which means

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{K}_{\hbar 2}^{\chi}g(x,v)\cdot h(x,v)\mathrm{d}v|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\iiint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}z_{-\alpha}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})(1+|v-v^{\prime}|)^{|\beta|+\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{(c\hbar+\vartheta)||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v^{\prime})|

\displaystyle\times|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}h(x,v)|\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})\chi(|v_{*}-v|)b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{I_{1}I_{2}}\,,

where

\displaystyle I_{1}=\iiint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}h(x,v)|^{2}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\mathrm{d}v

and

\displaystyle I_{2}=\iiint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)\nu^{-1}(v^{\prime})(1+|v-v^{\prime}|)^{2|\beta|+|m|(1-\gamma)}e^{2(c\hbar+\vartheta)||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v^{\prime})|^{2}

\displaystyle\times\mathfrak{M}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}(v_{*})\chi^{2}(|v-v_{*}|)b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\mathrm{d}v\,.

Due to
\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\lesssim\nu(v)
derived from Lemma 2.1 of
[
19
]
, the factor
I_{1}
can be bounded by

(8.67)

\displaystyle I_{1}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|h(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

Now we estimate the second factor
I_{2}
. Note that

\displaystyle\nu^{-1}(v^{\prime})\thicksim(1+|v^{\prime}|)^{-\gamma}\lesssim(1+|v_{*}^{\prime}|)^{|\gamma|}(1+|v^{\prime}-v_{*}^{\prime}|)^{-\gamma}\lesssim\mathfrak{M}^{-\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*}^{\prime})(1+|v^{\prime}-v_{*}^{\prime}|)^{-\gamma}

for
-3<\gamma\leq 1
. Moreover, the measure
\chi^{2}(|v_{*}-v|)b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\mathrm{d}v
is invariant under the transform
(v,v_{*})\mapsto(v^{\prime},v_{*}^{\prime})
. Then

\displaystyle I_{2}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathfrak{i}_{2}(v)\mathrm{d}v\,,

where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{i}_{2}(v)=\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v^{\prime})(1+|v-v_{*}|)^{-\gamma}(1+|v-v^{\prime}|)^{2|\beta|+|m|(1-\gamma)}e^{2(c\hbar+\vartheta)||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}

\displaystyle\times\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}(v_{*})\chi^{2}(|v-v_{*}|)b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

Let
V=v_{*}-v
,
V_{\shortparallel}=(V\cdot\omega)\omega
,
V_{\perp}=V-V_{\shortparallel}
. Then
\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}=2|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-2}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}
,
v^{\prime}=v+V_{\shortparallel}
,
v_{*}^{\prime}=v+V_{\perp}
,
v_{*}=v+V
. By (
8.21
) and (
8.24
), one has

\displaystyle-\tfrac{|v_{*}^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2T}=-\tfrac{|\zeta_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}{T}\,,\ |v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}=-2V_{\shortparallel}\cdot\zeta_{\shortparallel}\,,

where
\zeta=v+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}
,
\zeta_{\shortparallel}=[(\zeta-\mathfrak{u})\cdot\omega]\omega
,
\zeta_{\perp}=(\zeta-\mathfrak{u})-\zeta_{\shortparallel}
. Recall that

\displaystyle b(\omega,v_{*}-v)=|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\tilde{b}(\cos\theta)\leq C|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}|\cos\theta|=\tfrac{C|V_{\shortparallel}|}{|V|^{1-\gamma}}\,.

Then

\displaystyle\mathfrak{i}_{2}(v)\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v+V_{\shortparallel})(1+|V|)^{-\gamma}(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|)^{2|\beta|+|m|(1-\gamma)}e^{4(c\hbar+\vartheta)|V_{\shortparallel}\cdot\zeta_{\shortparallel}|}

\displaystyle\times e^{-\frac{3}{4T}(|\zeta_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2})}\tfrac{|V_{\shortparallel}|\chi^{2}(|V|)}{|V|^{1-\gamma}}2|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-2}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\,.

Note that
(1+|V|)^{-\gamma}\lesssim(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|)^{|\gamma|}(1+|V_{\perp}|)^{-\gamma}
. Then, for sufficiently small
\hbar,\vartheta\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{i}_{2}(v)\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v+V_{\shortparallel})|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}e^{-c(|\zeta_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2})}(1+|V_{\perp}|)^{-\gamma}\tfrac{\chi^{2}(|V|)}{|V|^{1-\gamma}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}

for some positive constant
c>0
. Observe that
\tfrac{\chi^{2}(|V|)}{|V|^{1-\gamma}}\lesssim(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}|^{2})^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}
. If
|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|>\frac{1}{2}|\zeta_{\perp}|
, one has
|V_{\perp}|\leq|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|+|\zeta_{\perp}|<3|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|
. Then
(1+|V_{\perp}|)^{-\gamma}\tfrac{\chi^{2}(|V|)}{|V|^{1-\gamma}}\lesssim e^{\frac{c}{2}|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}
. If
|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|\leq\frac{1}{2}|\zeta_{\perp}|
, it holds
|V_{\perp}|\geq|\zeta_{\perp}|-|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|\geq\tfrac{1}{2}|\zeta_{\perp}|
and
(1+|V_{\perp}|)^{-\gamma}\leq(1+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|)^{|\gamma|}(1+|\zeta_{\perp}|)^{1-\gamma}
, which imply that

\displaystyle(1+|V_{\perp}|)^{-\gamma}\tfrac{\chi^{2}(|V|)}{|V|^{1-\gamma}}\lesssim\tfrac{(1+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|)^{|\gamma|}(1+|\zeta_{\perp}|)^{1-\gamma}}{(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2})^{\frac{1-\gamma}{2}}}\lesssim e^{\frac{c}{2}|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\,.

In summary, the inequality
(1+|V_{\perp}|)^{-\gamma}\tfrac{\chi^{2}(|V|)}{|V|^{1-\gamma}}\lesssim e^{\frac{c}{2}|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}
holds. Then

\displaystyle\mathfrak{i}_{2}(v)\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v+V_{\shortparallel})e^{-c(|\zeta_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2})}\big(\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}e^{-\frac{c}{2}|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\big)\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v+V_{\shortparallel})e^{-c|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\,.

By the similar arguments in (
8.62
),
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v+V_{\shortparallel})e^{-c|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\lesssim 1
uniformly in
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, provided that
0\leq\alpha<\frac{1}{2}
. Then one has proved that
\mathfrak{i}_{2}(v)\lesssim 1
uniformly in
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
. It therefore infers that
I_{2}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v
, which, together with (
8.67
), implies that

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{K}_{\hbar 2}^{\chi}g(x,v)\cdot h(x,v)\mathrm{d}v|\lesssim\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|h(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

By letting
h(x,v)=\widetilde{K}_{\hbar 2}^{\chi}g(x,v)
, one concludes that for
0\leq\alpha<\frac{1}{2}
,

(8.68)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 2}^{\chi}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

Step 2.2. Control of
K_{\hbar 2}^{1-\chi}
.

Since
|v-v_{*}|\leq 2
in
K_{\hbar 2}^{1-\chi}
, the claim (
8.17
) shows
\mathfrak{M}(v_{*}^{\prime})\lesssim\mathfrak{M}^{1-\delta_{0}}(v^{\prime})\,,\ \mathfrak{M}(v_{*})\lesssim\mathfrak{M}^{1-\delta_{0}}(v)
for any fixed
\delta_{0}\in(0,1)
. Then

\displaystyle|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 2}^{1-\chi}g(x,v)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle z_{-\alpha}(v)\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\tfrac{w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v)\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)}}{w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v^{\prime})\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v^{\prime})e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v^{\prime})}}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1-\delta_{0}}{4}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1-\delta_{0}}{4}}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})

\displaystyle\times(1-\chi)(|v-v_{*}|)|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v^{\prime})|b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

By (
8.12
) and taking
\delta_{0}=\frac{1}{2}
, one has

\displaystyle\tfrac{w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v)\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)}}{w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v^{\prime})\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v^{\prime})e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v^{\prime})}}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1-\delta_{0}}{4}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1-\delta_{0}}{4}}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})\lesssim[\mathfrak{M}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}(v_{*})\mathfrak{M}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}(v)]^{\frac{1}{16}}

for sufficiently small
\hbar,\vartheta\geq 0
. It thereby holds

(8.69)

\displaystyle|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 2}^{1-\chi}g(x,v)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v^{\prime})|z_{-\alpha}(v)[\mathfrak{M}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}(v_{*})\mathfrak{M}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}(v)]^{\frac{1}{16}}b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

Let
V=v_{*}-v
,
V_{\shortparallel}=(V\cdot\omega)\omega
,
V_{\perp}=V-V_{\shortparallel}
,
\zeta=v+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}
,
\zeta_{\shortparallel}=[(\zeta-\mathfrak{u})\cdot\omega]\omega
,
\zeta_{\perp}=(\zeta-\mathfrak{u})-\zeta_{\shortparallel}
. Then
\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}=2|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-2}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}
,
v^{\prime}=v+V_{\shortparallel}
,
v_{*}^{\prime}=v+V_{\perp}
,
v_{*}=v+V
. Denote by
\tilde{g}(x,v^{\prime})=\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v^{\prime})
. It then follows from (
1.4
)-(
1.5
)-(
8.21
) and
|v+V_{\shortparallel}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}=|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|\zeta_{\shortparallel}+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}
that

\displaystyle|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 2}^{1-\chi}g(x,v)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}z_{-\alpha}(v)\tilde{g}(x,v+V_{\shortparallel})[\mathfrak{M}(v+V_{\perp})\mathfrak{M}(v+V)\mathfrak{M}(v+V_{\shortparallel})\mathfrak{M}(v)]^{\frac{1}{16}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 17.00024pt\times(1-\chi)(|V|)b(\omega,V)\cdot 2|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-2}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle z_{-\alpha}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{16}}(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}\tilde{g}(v+V_{\shortparallel})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{32}}(v+V_{\shortparallel})

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 8.50012pt\times e^{-\frac{1}{32T}(|\zeta_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|\zeta_{\shortparallel}+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}|^{2})}\tfrac{|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}(1-\chi)(|V|)}{|V|^{1-\gamma}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle z_{-\alpha}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{16}}(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}\tilde{g}(x,v+V_{\shortparallel})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{32}}(v+V_{\shortparallel})

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 8.50012pt\times e^{-\frac{1}{32T}(\frac{1}{4}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|V_{\perp}|^{2})}\tfrac{|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}}{(|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}|^{2})^{\frac{1-\gamma}{2}}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}

(8.70)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle z_{-\alpha}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{16}}(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{g}(x,v+V_{\shortparallel})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{32}}(v+V_{\shortparallel})|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}e^{-\frac{|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}{128T}}\Phi(V_{\shortparallel})\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\,,

where

\displaystyle\Phi(V_{\shortparallel})=\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}(|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}|^{2})^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}e^{-\frac{|V_{\perp}|^{2}}{64T}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\,.

By Lemma 2.3 of
[
18
]
,
(|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}|^{2})^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}|V_{\perp}|^{b_{0}}\lesssim|V_{\shortparallel}|^{b_{0}+\gamma-1}
holds for any fixed
b_{0}\in[0,1-\gamma]
. Then, if
0\leq b_{0}\leq 1-\gamma
and
b_{0}<2
,

\displaystyle\Phi(|V_{\shortparallel}|)\lesssim|V_{\shortparallel}|^{b_{0}+\gamma-1}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}|V_{\perp}|^{-b_{0}}e^{-\frac{|V_{\perp}|^{2}}{64T}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\lesssim|V_{\shortparallel}|^{b_{0}+\gamma-1}\,.

It therefore infers that

\displaystyle|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 2}^{1-\chi}g(x,v)|\lesssim

\displaystyle z_{-\alpha}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{32}}(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{g}(x,v+V_{\shortparallel})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{32}}(v+V_{\shortparallel})|V_{\shortparallel}|^{b_{0}+\gamma-2}e^{-\frac{|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}{128T}}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}

(8.71)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle z_{-\alpha}(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{g}(x,\xi)\mathfrak{k}_{b_{0}}(v,\xi)\mathrm{d}\xi\,,

where
\xi=v+V_{\shortparallel}
has been used, and

\displaystyle\mathfrak{k}_{b_{0}}(v,\xi)=[\mathfrak{M}(v)\mathfrak{M}(\xi)]^{\frac{1}{32}}|v-\xi|^{b_{0}+\gamma-2}e^{-\frac{|v-\xi|^{2}}{128T}}

with
b_{0}<2
and
0\leq b_{0}\leq 1-\gamma
. We further require
b_{0}+\gamma-2>-3
such that the kernel
\mathfrak{k}_{b_{0}}(v,\xi)
is integrable on the variables both
\xi
and
v
. More precisely, one has

(8.72)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathfrak{k}_{b_{0}}(v,\xi)\mathrm{d}\xi=\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{32}}(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-\xi|^{b_{0}+\gamma-2}e^{-\frac{|v-\xi|^{2}}{128T}}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{32}}(\xi)\mathrm{d}\xi\lesssim(1+|v|)^{b_{0}+\gamma-2}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{32}}(v)\lesssim 1

uniformly in
v
by Lemma 2.1 of
[
19
]
. Furthermore, by (
8.61
),

(8.73)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)\mathfrak{k}_{b_{0}}(v,\xi)\mathrm{d}v=\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{32}}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)|v-\xi|^{b_{0}+\gamma-2}e^{-\frac{|v-\xi|^{2}}{128T}}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{32}}(v)\mathrm{d}\xi\lesssim\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{32}}(\xi)(1+|\xi|)^{b_{0}+\gamma-2}\lesssim 1

uniformly in
\xi
, provided that
b_{0}+\gamma-2>-3
and
0\leq\alpha<\min\{\frac{1}{2},\frac{b_{0}+\gamma+1}{2}\}
with
b_{0}\in S_{\gamma}
. It thereby follows from (
8.72
)-(
8.73
) that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 2}^{1-\chi}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)\big(\tilde{g}(x,\xi)\mathfrak{k}_{b_{0}}(v,\xi)\mathrm{d}\xi\big)^{2}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathfrak{k}_{b_{0}}(v,\xi)\mathrm{d}\xi\big)\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\tilde{g}(x,\xi)|^{2}\mathfrak{k}_{b_{0}}(v,\xi)\mathrm{d}\xi\big)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle{\mathrel{\to}^{\text{Fubini Theorem}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathfrak{k}_{b_{0}}(v,\xi)\mathrm{d}\xi\big)\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)\mathfrak{k}_{b_{0}}(v,\xi)\mathrm{d}\xi\big)|\tilde{g}(x,\xi)|^{2}\mathrm{d}\xi

(8.74)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\tilde{g}(x,\xi)|^{2}\mathrm{d}\xi=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v

for
0\leq\alpha<\min\{\frac{1}{2},\frac{b_{0}+\gamma+1}{2}\}
with
-3<\gamma\leq 1
and
b_{0}\in S_{\gamma}
. Therefore, the bounds (
8.68
) and (
8.74
) conclude the claim (
8.64
).

Step 3. Estimates for
K_{\hbar 3}
part.

The goal is to show that for
0\leq\alpha<\min\{\frac{1}{2},\frac{b_{1}+\gamma}{2}\}
with
b_{1}\in T_{\gamma}
given in Lemma
2.5
,

(8.75)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 3}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

As similar as in (
8.65
),
K_{\hbar 3}g(x,v)
can be split as

\displaystyle K_{\hbar 3}g(x,v)=K_{\hbar 3}^{\chi}g(x,v)+K_{\hbar 3}^{1-\chi}g(x,v)\,,

where for
a=\chi
or
1-\chi

\displaystyle K_{\hbar 3}^{a}g(x,v)=\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,v_{*}^{\prime})}a(|v-v_{*}|)g(x,v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

Step 3.1. Control of
K_{\hbar 3}^{\chi}
.

By (
8.66
) with
v^{\prime}
replaced by
v_{*}^{\prime}
, one has

\displaystyle|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 3}g(x,v)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle z_{-\alpha}(v)\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}(1+|v-v_{*}^{\prime}|)^{|\beta|+\frac{|m|(1-\gamma)}{2}}e^{(c\hbar+\vartheta)||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v_{*}^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*}^{\prime})

\displaystyle\times|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v_{*}^{\prime})|\chi(|v-v_{*}|)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{*})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,,

which implies that by the Hölder inequality

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 3}^{\chi}g(x,v)\cdot h(x,v)\mathrm{d}v|\lesssim\sqrt{I\!I_{1}I\!I_{2}}\,,

where

\displaystyle I\!I_{1}=\iiint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)h(x,v)|^{2}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*})b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\mathrm{d}v\,,

and

\displaystyle I\!I_{2}=\iiint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)\nu^{-1}(v_{*}^{\prime})(1+|v-v_{*}^{\prime}|)^{2|\beta|+|m|(1-\gamma)}e^{2(c\hbar+\vartheta)||v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v_{*}^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}

\displaystyle\times|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}\mathfrak{M}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}(v_{*})\chi^{2}(|v-v_{*}|)b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\mathrm{d}v\,.

By (
8.67
), one has

(8.76)

\displaystyle I\!I_{1}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|h(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

From changing the variables
(v,v^{\prime})\mapsto(v_{*},v_{*}^{\prime})
and employing the Fubini Theorem, it follows that

\displaystyle I\!I_{2}=\iiint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v_{*})\nu^{-1}(v^{\prime})(1+|v_{*}-v^{\prime}|)^{2|\beta|+|m|(1-\gamma)}e^{2(c\hbar+\vartheta)||v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}

\displaystyle\times|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v^{\prime})|^{2}\mathfrak{M}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}(v)\chi^{2}(|v-v_{*}|)b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\mathrm{d}v\,,

where we have used the fact that the measure
\chi^{2}(|v-v_{*}|)b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\mathrm{d}v
is invariant under the previous changing variables. Further by changing variables
(v,v_{*})\mapsto(v^{\prime},v_{*}^{\prime})
and the corresponding invariant of
\chi^{2}(|v-v_{*}|)b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\mathrm{d}v
, one has

\displaystyle I\!I_{2}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathfrak{j}_{2}(v)\mathrm{d}v\,,

where the kernel

\displaystyle\mathfrak{j}_{2}(v)=\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})\nu^{-1}(v)(1+|v_{*}^{\prime}-v|)^{2|\beta|+|m|(1-\gamma)}e^{2(c\hbar+\vartheta)||v_{*}^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}|}

\displaystyle\times\mathfrak{M}(v_{*})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}(v^{\prime})\chi^{2}(|v-v_{*}|)b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

We now want to show that
\mathfrak{j}_{2}(v)\lesssim 1
uniformly in
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
.

Let
V=v_{*}-v
,
V_{\shortparallel}=(V\cdot\omega)\omega
,
V_{\perp}=V-V_{\shortparallel}
,
\zeta=v+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}
,
\zeta_{\shortparallel}=[(\zeta-\mathfrak{u})\cdot\omega]\omega
,
\zeta_{\perp}=(\zeta-\mathfrak{u})-\zeta_{\shortparallel}
. Then
\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}=2|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-2}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}
,
v^{\prime}=v+V_{\shortparallel}
,
v_{*}^{\prime}=v+V_{\perp}
,
v_{*}=v+V
. Note that

\displaystyle-\tfrac{|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2T}=-\tfrac{2|\zeta_{\shortparallel}+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}{2T}\,,\ |v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|v_{*}^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}=|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}-|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}\,.

Moreover,
\nu^{-1}(v)\thicksim(1+|v|)^{-\gamma}\lesssim(1+|v^{\prime}|)^{|\gamma|}(1+|v-v^{\prime}|)^{-\gamma}
for
-3<\gamma\leq 1
, which means that
\nu^{-1}(v)\mathfrak{M}(v_{*})\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}(v^{\prime})\lesssim(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|)^{-\gamma}e^{-\frac{|v^{\prime}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|v_{*}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{4T}}
. Together with (
1.4
)-(
1.5
), one has

\displaystyle\mathfrak{j}_{2}(v)\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v+V_{\perp})(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|)^{-\gamma}(1+|V_{\perp}|)^{2|\beta|+|m|(1-\gamma)}e^{2(c\hbar+\vartheta)||\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}-|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}|}

\displaystyle\times e^{-\frac{2|\zeta_{\shortparallel}+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}{4T}}\tfrac{\chi^{2}(|V|)|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}}{|V|^{1-\gamma}}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\,.

For sufficiently small
\hbar\geq 0
, there is a
c^{\prime}>0
such that

\displaystyle(1+|V_{\perp}|)^{2|\beta|+|m|(1-\gamma)}e^{2(c\hbar+\vartheta)||\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}-|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}|}e^{-\frac{|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}{4T}}\lesssim e^{-c^{\prime}(|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}|^{2})}\,.

It also holds
\tfrac{(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|)^{-\gamma}\chi^{2}(|V|)}{|V|^{1-\gamma}}\lesssim\tfrac{(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|)^{-\gamma}}{(1+|V_{\shortparallel}|+|V_{\perp}|)^{1-\gamma}}\lesssim 1
for
-3<\gamma\leq 1
. Then

\displaystyle\mathfrak{j}_{2}(v)\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}e^{-\frac{|\zeta_{\shortparallel}+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}{2T}}\Big(\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v+V_{\perp})e^{-c^{\prime}(|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}|^{2})}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\Big)\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\,.

Observe that for
0\leq\alpha<\frac{1}{2}
,
\int_{V_{\perp}\perp V_{\shortparallel}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v+V_{\perp})e^{-c^{\prime}(|\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}|^{2})}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\lesssim 1
uniformly in
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Then
\mathfrak{j}_{2}(v)\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-1}e^{-\frac{|\zeta_{\shortparallel}+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}{2T}}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\lesssim 1
, where the last inequality is derived from (
8.33
)-(
8.34
). It thereby follows

(8.77)

\displaystyle I\!I_{2}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

As a result, (
8.76
) and (
8.77
) indicate that

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 3}^{\chi}g(x,v)\cdot h(x,v)\mathrm{d}v|\lesssim\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|h(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,,

which, by taking
h(x,v)=\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 3}^{\chi}g(x,v)
, implies that

(8.78)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 3}^{\chi}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v

for
-3<\gamma\leq 1
,
m\in\mathbb{R}
and
0\leq\alpha<\frac{1}{2}
.

Step 3.2. Control of
K_{\hbar 3}^{1-\chi}
.

Due to
|v-v_{*}|\leq 2
in
K_{\hbar 3}^{1-\chi}g(x,v)
, the claim (
8.17
) reads
\mathfrak{M}(v^{\prime})\lesssim\mathfrak{M}^{1-\delta_{0}}(v_{*}^{\prime})
and
\mathfrak{M}(v_{*})\lesssim\mathfrak{M}^{1-\delta_{0}}(v)
for any fixed
\delta_{0}\in(0,1)
. From the similar arguments in (
8.69
), it follows that

\displaystyle|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 3}^{1-\chi}g(x,v)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v_{*}^{\prime})|z_{-\alpha}(v)[\mathfrak{M}(v_{*}^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}(v_{*})\mathfrak{M}(v^{\prime})\mathfrak{M}(v)]^{\frac{1}{16}}b(\omega,v_{*}-v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}\,.

for sufficiently small
\hbar,\vartheta\geq 0
. Let
V=v_{*}-v
,
V_{\shortparallel}=(V\cdot\omega)\omega
,
V_{\perp}=V-V_{\shortparallel}
,
\zeta=v+\frac{1}{2}V_{\shortparallel}
,
\zeta_{\shortparallel}=[(\zeta-\mathfrak{u})\cdot\omega]\omega
,
\zeta_{\perp}=(\zeta-\mathfrak{u})-\zeta_{\shortparallel}
. Then one has
\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}=2|V_{\perp}|^{-2}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}
. We remark that the derivation of the previous relation is similar to that of
\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}v_{*}=2|V_{\shortparallel}|^{-2}\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}
given in (38), Page 35 of
[
12
]
. It therefore infers from the similar arguments in (
8.70
) that

\displaystyle|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 3}^{1-\chi}g(x,v)|

\displaystyle\lesssim z_{-\alpha}(v)\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{16}}(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v+V_{\perp})|\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{16}}(v+V_{\perp})|V_{\perp}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|V_{\perp}|^{2}}{64T}}\Psi(V_{\perp})\mathrm{d}V_{\perp}\,,

where

\displaystyle\Psi(V_{\perp})=\int_{V_{\shortparallel}\perp V_{\perp}}e^{-\frac{1}{32T}(|\zeta_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2})}\tfrac{|V_{\shortparallel}|(1-\chi)(|V|)}{(|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}|^{2})^{\frac{1-\gamma}{2}}}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\,.

By Lemma 2.3 of
[
18
]
,
(|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|V_{\perp}|^{2})^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{b_{1}}\lesssim|V_{\perp}|^{b_{1}+\gamma-1}
holds for any fixed
b_{1}\in[0,1-\gamma]
. Then

\displaystyle\Psi(V_{\perp})\lesssim|V_{\perp}|^{b_{1}+\gamma-1}\int_{V_{\shortparallel}\perp V_{\perp}}e^{-\frac{|V_{\shortparallel}|^{2}}{128T}}|V_{\shortparallel}|^{1-b_{1}}\mathrm{d}V_{\shortparallel}\lesssim|V_{\perp}|^{b_{1}+\gamma-1}

under the further constraint
b_{1}<3
. It thereby follows from the same arguments in (
8.71
) that

\displaystyle|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 3}^{1-\chi}g(x,v)|\lesssim z_{-\alpha}(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,\varpi)|\mathfrak{k}_{b_{1}}(v,\varpi)\mathrm{d}\varpi\,,

where
\mathfrak{k}_{b_{1}}(v,\varpi)=[\mathfrak{M}(v)\mathfrak{M}(\varpi)]^{\frac{1}{32}}|v-\varpi|^{b_{1}+\gamma-3}e^{-\frac{|v-\varpi|^{2}}{128T}}
with
b_{1}<3
and
0\leq b_{1}\leq 1-\gamma
. We further assume that
b_{1}+\gamma-3>-3
. As similar as in (
8.72
)-(
8.73
), one has

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathfrak{k}_{b_{1}}(v,\varpi)\mathrm{d}\varpi\lesssim(1+|v|)^{b_{1}+\gamma-3}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{32}}(v)\lesssim 1

uniformly in
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, and

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)\mathfrak{k}_{b_{1}}(v,\varpi)\mathrm{d}v\lesssim(1+|\varpi|)^{b_{1}+\gamma-3}\mathfrak{M}^{\frac{1}{32}}(\varpi)\lesssim 1

uniformly in
\varpi\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, provided that
0\leq\alpha<\min\{\frac{1}{2},\frac{b_{1}+\gamma}{2}\}
with
-3<\gamma\leq 1
and
b_{1}\in\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}
. Then the similar arguments in (
8.74
) shows that

(8.79)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}K_{\hbar 3}^{1-\chi}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v

for
0\leq\alpha<\min\{\frac{1}{2},\frac{b_{1}+\gamma}{2}\}
with
-3<\gamma\leq 1
and
b_{1}\in\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}
. As a result, the bounds (
8.78
) and (
8.78
) imply the claim (
8.75
).

Finally, the inequalities (
8.60
), (
8.64
) and (
8.75
) conclude the bound (
2.12
). Then the proof of Lemma
2.5
is completed.
∎

9.
Properties of artificial damping operator
\mathbf{D}

The goal of this section is to study the properties of artificial damping operator
\mathbf{D}
, i.e., to prove Lemma
2.6
-
2.7
-
2.8
-
2.9
.

9.1.
Coercivity of
\mathbf{D}

We now study the coercivity of the artificial damping operator
\mathbf{D}
, hence, to verify Lemma
2.6
.

Proof of Lemma
2.6
.

We first decompose the left quantity in (
2.14
) as

(9.1)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}^{2}g(\mathbf{D}_{\hbar}g-\hbar\sigma_{x}v_{3}g)\mathrm{d}v=D_{0}+D_{1}+D_{2}\,,

where

(9.2)

\displaystyle D_{0}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big[w_{\beta,\vartheta}^{2}g\mathbf{D}_{\hbar}g-\hbar\sigma_{x}v_{3}(\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)^{2}\big]\mathrm{d}v\,,\ D_{1}=-\hbar\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sigma_{x}v_{3}(\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,,

\displaystyle D_{2}

\displaystyle=-2\hbar\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sigma_{x}v_{3}\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\cdot\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathrm{d}v\,.

Due to
|v_{3}\sigma_{x}|\leq c\nu(v)
as in Lemma
2.1
, the quantity
D_{1}
can be bounded by

(9.3)

\displaystyle|D_{1}|\leq c\hbar\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu(v)|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,,

and
D_{2}
can be dominated by

\displaystyle|D_{2}|\leq

\displaystyle c\hbar\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\sigma_{x}v_{3}||\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|\,|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c\hbar\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)|v_{3}\sigma_{x}||\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu(v)|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

Moreover, one has

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}|v_{3}\sigma_{x}||\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v=\underbrace{\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}\leq\delta x+l}(\cdots)\mathrm{d}v}_{:=D_{21}}+\underbrace{\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}>\delta x+l}(\cdots)\mathrm{d}v}_{:=D_{22}}\,.

By (
8.1
)-(
8.2
) below, if
2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}\leq\delta x+l
, it follows that
\sigma_{x}=\tfrac{10\delta}{3-\gamma}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}
. Furthermore, the definition of
\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g
in (
1.13
) can be rewritten as

(9.4)

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g=\sum_{j=0}^{4}a_{j}\psi_{j}\,,\ \int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\psi_{i}(v)\psi_{j}(v)\mathrm{d}v=\delta_{ij}\quad(0\leq i,j\leq 4)\,,

which means that

(9.5)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v=\sum_{j=0}^{4}a_{j}^{2}\,.

It is easy to see that
\psi_{j}
satisfy

(9.6)

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{4}\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)|\psi_{j}(v)|\leq c^{\prime\prime}e^{-c^{\prime}|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}

for some
c^{\prime},c^{\prime\prime}>0
. Then
D_{21}
can be bounded by

\displaystyle D_{21}\leq C\delta(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}\leq\delta x+l}|v_{3}|e^{-c^{\prime}|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}v\sum_{j=0}^{4}a_{j}^{2}\leq C\delta(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

Due to
|v_{3}\sigma_{x}|\leq c\nu(v)
in Lemma
2.1
and the relations (
9.4
)-(
9.5
)-(
9.6
),
D_{22}
can be controlled by

\displaystyle D_{22}\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}>\delta x+l}\nu(v)e^{-c^{\prime}|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}v\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\frac{c^{\prime}}{8}(\frac{\delta x+l}{2})^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu(v)e^{-\frac{c^{\prime}}{2}|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}\mathrm{d}v\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v

for some
c_{0}>0
, where we have used the fact
|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}\geq\tfrac{1}{4}(\tfrac{\delta x+l}{2})^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}
derived from
2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}>\delta x+l
when
l\geq 2^{4-\gamma}
. Then one has
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}|v_{3}\sigma_{x}||\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\leq C(\delta+e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}})(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v
, which yields that

(9.7)

\displaystyle|D_{2}|\leq C\hbar^{\frac{3}{2}}(\delta+e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}})(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v+C\hbar^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu(v)|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

It turns to control the quantity
D_{0}
in (
9.2
). Observe that

(9.8)

\displaystyle D_{0}=E+G\,,

where

(9.9)

\displaystyle E=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big[w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathbf{D}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g-\hbar\sigma_{x}v_{3}(\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)^{2}\big]\mathrm{d}v\,,\ G=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(w_{\beta,\vartheta}\mathbf{D}_{\hbar}w_{\beta,\vartheta}^{-1}-\mathbf{D})w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathrm{d}v\,.

We split the quantity
E
as

(9.10)

\displaystyle E=\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}\leq\delta x+l}(\cdots)\mathrm{d}v+\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}>\delta x+l}(\cdots)\mathrm{d}v:=E_{1}+E_{2}\,.

We first control the quantity
E_{1}
. By utilizing the fact (
1.16
), one knows that

(9.11)

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g=\sum_{j=0}^{4}a_{j}^{*}\psi_{j}^{*}:=\sum_{j=0}^{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\psi_{j}^{*}\mathrm{d}v\psi_{j}^{*}\,.

By (
8.1
), if
2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}\leq\delta x+l
, one has
\sigma_{x}=\tfrac{10\delta}{3-\gamma}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}
. Together with the definition of
\mathbf{D}
in (
1.63
), the quantity
E_{1}
can be expressed by

(9.12)

\displaystyle E_{1}=(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}\leq\delta x+l}\big[\ss_{+}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{+}(v_{3}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)+\ss_{0}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{0}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g

\displaystyle-\tfrac{10\delta\hbar}{3-\gamma}v_{3}\big\{\big(\sum_{j=0}^{4}a_{j}^{*}\psi_{j}^{*}\big)^{2}-(a_{4}^{*}\psi_{4}^{*})^{2}\big\}+v_{3}(a_{4}^{*}\psi_{4}^{*})^{2}\big]\mathrm{d}v\,.

Observe that

(9.13)

\displaystyle\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}\leq\delta x+l}\ss_{+}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{+}(v_{3}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\ss_{+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{+}(v_{3}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v}_{E_{10}}-\underbrace{\ss_{+}\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}>\delta x+l}\ss_{+}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{+}(v_{3}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v}_{E_{11}}\,.

It then follows from the straightforward computation that

(9.14)

\displaystyle E_{10}=

\displaystyle\ss_{+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{+}(v_{3}\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v+\ss_{+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{+}(v_{3}\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ss_{+}(a_{3}^{*})^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\psi_{3}^{*}\mathcal{P}v_{3}\mathcal{P}\psi_{3}^{*}\mathrm{d}v+\ss_{+}a_{3}^{*}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\psi_{3}^{*}\mathcal{P}^{+}(v_{3}\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\tfrac{5}{3}T}\ss_{+}(a_{3}^{*})^{2}+\ss_{+}a_{3}^{*}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\psi_{3}^{*}\mathcal{P}^{+}(v_{3}\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\sqrt{\tfrac{5}{3}T}\ss_{+}(a_{3}^{*})^{2}-C\ss_{+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

By the definition of
\mathcal{P}^{+}
in (
1.17
), one has

\displaystyle E_{11}=\ss_{+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{3}\psi_{3}^{*}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathrm{d}v\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}>\delta x+l}\psi_{3}^{*}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathrm{d}v\,.

Notice that
|\psi_{3}^{*}|\leq Ce^{-c_{0}(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{2}}
for some
C,c_{0}>0
. Then

\displaystyle|\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}>\delta x+l}\psi_{3}^{*}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathrm{d}v|\leq

\displaystyle e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\frac{c_{0}}{2}(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{2}}|\psi_{3}^{*}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu|w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

One obviously has
|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{3}\psi_{3}^{*}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathrm{d}v|\leq C\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu|w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}
. Then the quantity
E_{11}
can be bounded by

(9.15)

\displaystyle E_{11}\leq C\ss_{+}e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\big)\,.

In summary, the relations (
9.13
), (
9.14
) and (
9.15
) reduce to

\displaystyle\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}\leq\delta x+l}\ss_{+}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{+}(v_{3}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\sqrt{\tfrac{5}{3}T}\ss_{+}(a_{3}^{*})^{2}-C\ss_{+}e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v

(9.16)

\displaystyle-C\ss_{+}(1+e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}\leq\delta x+l}\ss_{0}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{0}(v_{3}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ss_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{0}(v_{3}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v-\ss_{0}\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}>\delta x+l}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{0}(v_{3}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v\,.

By Lemma 3 of
[
7
]
, the map
w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mapsto\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{0}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathrm{d}v\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}
defines a norm on
\mathrm{Span}\{\psi_{0}^{*},\psi_{1}^{*},\psi_{2}^{*}\}
. It is well known that all norms on the finite dimensional space are equivalent each other. Then there is a constant
\mu_{1}>0
such that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{0}(v_{3}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v\geq\mu_{1}\sum_{j=0}^{2}(a_{j}^{*})^{2}\,.

Following the similar arguments in (
9.15
), one has

\displaystyle\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}>\delta x+l}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{0}(v_{3}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v\leq Ce^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\big)\,.

It therefore holds that

(9.17)

\displaystyle\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}\leq\delta x+l}\ss_{0}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g\mathcal{P}^{0}(v_{3}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mu_{1}\ss_{0}\sum_{j=0}^{2}(a_{j}^{*})^{2}-C\ss_{0}e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\big)\,.

Recall that
\psi_{4}^{*}
satisfies
\mathcal{A}\psi_{4}^{*}=\mathcal{P}v_{3}\mathcal{P}\psi_{4}^{*}=\lambda_{4}\psi_{4}^{*}
with
\lambda_{4}=-\sqrt{\frac{5}{3}T}<0
. Then

(9.18)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{10\delta\hbar}{3-\gamma}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{3}(a_{4}^{*}\psi_{4}^{*})^{2}\mathrm{d}v=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\tfrac{3}{5T}}\tfrac{10\delta\hbar}{3-\gamma}(a_{4}^{*})^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{3}\psi_{4}^{*}\mathcal{P}v_{3}\mathcal{P}\psi_{4}^{*}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\tfrac{3}{5T}}\tfrac{10\delta\hbar}{3-\gamma}(a_{4}^{*})^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\mathcal{P}v_{3}\psi_{4}^{*})^{2}\mathrm{d}v\geq\mu_{2}\delta\hbar(a_{4}^{*})^{2}

with
\mu_{2}=\sqrt{\tfrac{3}{5T}}\tfrac{10}{3-\gamma}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\mathcal{P}v_{3}\psi_{4}^{*})^{2}\mathrm{d}v>0
. Furthermore, the similar arguments in (
9.15
) imply that

\displaystyle|\tfrac{10\delta\hbar}{3-\gamma}\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}>\delta x+l}v_{3}(a_{4}^{*}\psi_{4}^{*})^{2}\mathrm{d}v|\leq C\delta\hbar e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

As a consequence,

(9.19)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{10\delta\hbar}{3-\gamma}\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}\leq\delta x+l}v_{3}(a_{4}^{*}\psi_{4}^{*})^{2}\mathrm{d}v\geq\mu_{2}\delta\hbar(a_{4}^{*})^{2}-C\delta\hbar e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

Following the above similar arguments, one derives that

(9.20)

\displaystyle|\tfrac{10\delta\hbar}{3-\gamma}\int_{2(1+|v-\mathfrak{u}|)^{3-\gamma}\leq\delta x+l}v_{3}\big\{\big(\sum_{j=0}^{4}a_{j}^{*}\psi_{j}^{*}\big)^{2}-(a_{4}^{*}\psi_{4}^{*})^{2}\big\}\mathrm{d}v|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\mu_{2}\delta\hbar(a_{4}^{*})^{2}+c_{1}\delta\hbar\sum_{j=0}^{3}(a_{j}^{*})^{2}+C\delta\hbar e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v

for some harmless constant
c_{1}>0
. Consequently, combining with the relations (
9.12
), (
9.16
), (
9.17
), (
9.19
) and (
9.20
), one gains

\displaystyle E_{1}\geq

\displaystyle(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\big[(\mu_{1}\ss_{0}-c_{1}\delta\hbar)\sum_{j=0}^{2}(a_{j}^{*})^{2}+(\tfrac{1}{2}\sqrt{\tfrac{5}{3}T}\ss_{+}-c_{1}\delta\hbar)(a_{3}^{*})^{2}+\tfrac{1}{2}\mu_{2}\delta\hbar(a_{4}^{*})^{2}\big]

\displaystyle-C(\ss_{0}+\ss_{+}+\delta\hbar)e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle-C[\ss_{0}+\ss_{+}(1+e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}})](\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

We now take
\ss_{0}=\ss_{0}^{\prime}\delta\hbar
and
\ss_{+}=\ss_{+}^{\prime}\delta\hbar
, where
\ss_{0}^{\prime},\ss_{+}^{\prime}>0
are large enough such that

\displaystyle\mu_{1}\ss_{0}^{\prime}-c_{1}\geq\tfrac{1}{2}\mu_{2}\,,\ \tfrac{1}{2}\sqrt{\tfrac{5}{3}T}\ss_{+}^{\prime}-c_{1}\geq\tfrac{1}{2}\mu_{2}\,.

Then, together with the properties of the orthonormal basis
\{\psi_{j}^{*}\}_{0\leq j\leq 4}
in (
1.16
) and the definitions of
a_{j}^{*}(0\leq j\leq 4)
in (
9.11
), one obtains

\displaystyle(\mu_{1}\ss_{0}-c_{1}\delta\hbar)\sum_{j=0}^{2}(a_{j}^{*})^{2}+(\tfrac{1}{2}\sqrt{\tfrac{5}{3}T}\ss_{+}-c_{1}\delta\hbar)(a_{3}^{*})^{2}+\tfrac{1}{2}\mu_{2}\delta\hbar(a_{4}^{*})^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\mu_{2}\delta\hbar\sum_{j=0}^{4}(a_{j}^{*})^{2}=\tfrac{1}{2}\mu_{2}\delta\hbar\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,,

which means that

(9.21)
