\displaystyle E_{1}\geq(5\mu_{0}-Ce^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}})\delta\hbar(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v-C\delta\hbar(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,,

where
\mu_{0}=\tfrac{1}{10}\mu_{2}>0
.

By the similar arguments in (
9.15
), the quantity
E_{2}
can be bounded by

(9.22)

\displaystyle|E_{2}|\leq Ce^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\hbar(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

Then (
9.10
), (
9.21
) and (
9.22
) show that

(9.23)

\displaystyle E\geq E_{1}-|E_{2}|\geq\big[5\mu_{0}\delta-C(\delta+1)e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\big]\hbar(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle-C\delta\hbar(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

We then control the quantity
G
. By the definition of the operator
\mathbf{D}
in (
1.63
), one has

(9.24)

\displaystyle(w_{\beta,\vartheta}\mathbf{D}_{\hbar}w_{\beta,\vartheta}^{-1}-\mathbf{D})w_{\beta,\vartheta}g=

\displaystyle(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\Big\{\ss_{+}\big[\underbrace{w_{\beta,\vartheta}e^{\hbar\sigma}\mathcal{P}^{+}(v_{3}w_{\beta,\vartheta}^{-1}e^{-\hbar\sigma}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)-\mathcal{P}^{+}(v_{3}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)}_{G_{1}}\big]

\displaystyle+\ss_{0}\big[\underbrace{w_{\beta,\vartheta}e^{\hbar\sigma}\mathcal{P}^{0}(w_{\beta,\vartheta}^{-1}e^{-\hbar\sigma}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)-\mathcal{P}^{0}(w_{\beta,\vartheta}g)}_{G_{2}}\big]\Big\}\,.

Together with the definition of
\mathcal{P}^{+}
in (
1.17
) and the fact
w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v)w_{\beta,\vartheta}^{-1}(\tilde{v})\leq Ce^{2\vartheta(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|\tilde{v}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})}
, one has

\displaystyle|G_{1}|\leq C|\psi_{3}^{*}(v)|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\tilde{v}_{3}|\mathfrak{q}(\hbar,\vartheta,v,\tilde{v})|w_{\beta,\vartheta}(\tilde{v})g(\tilde{v})\psi_{3}^{*}(\tilde{v})|\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\,,

where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{q}(\hbar,\vartheta,v,\tilde{v})=e^{2\vartheta(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|\tilde{v}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})+\hbar|\sigma(x,v)-\sigma(x,\tilde{v})|}-1\,.

By the similar arguments in Lemma 2 of
[
3
]
, one knows that for
0\leq\vartheta<\tfrac{1}{16T}
,

(9.25)

\displaystyle\sup_{v,\tilde{v}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big\{\mathfrak{q}(\hbar,\vartheta,v,\tilde{v})e^{-\frac{|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|\tilde{v}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{8T}}\big\}\leq C\hbar

for sufficiently small
\hbar>0
and for some harmless constant
C>0
. Then
G_{1}
can be further bounded by

(9.26)

\displaystyle|G_{1}|\leq C\hbar|\psi_{3}^{*}(v)|\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu(\tilde{v})|w_{\beta,\vartheta}(\tilde{v})g(\tilde{v})|^{2}\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

Similarly,
G_{2}
can be bounded by

(9.27)

\displaystyle|G_{2}|\leq C\hbar\sum_{j=0}^{2}|\psi_{j}^{*}(v)|\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu(\tilde{v})|w_{\beta,\vartheta}(\tilde{v})g(\tilde{v})|^{2}\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

From plugging (
9.24
), (
9.26
) and (
9.27
) into the definition of
G
in (
9.9
), one follows that

(9.28)

\displaystyle|G|\leq C\delta\hbar^{2}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\big)

for
0\leq\vartheta<\tfrac{1}{16T}
and sufficiently small
\hbar>0
. It then follows from (
9.8
), (
9.23
) and (
9.28
) that

\displaystyle D_{0}\geq

\displaystyle E-|G|\geq\big[5\mu_{0}\delta-C\delta\hbar-C(\delta+1)e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\big]\hbar(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v

(9.29)

\displaystyle-C\delta\hbar(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

Then, by plugging (
9.3
), (
9.7
) and (
9.29
) into (
9.1
), one has

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}^{2}g(\mathbf{D}_{\hbar}g-\hbar\sigma_{x}v_{3}g)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\big[5\mu_{0}\delta-C\delta\hbar-C\delta\hbar^{\frac{1}{2}}-C(\delta+\hbar^{\frac{1}{2}}+1)e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\big]\hbar(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{P}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle-C(\delta\hbar+\hbar+\hbar^{\frac{1}{2}})(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nu|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

We first take
0<\delta<1
and
0<\hbar<\frac{2\mu_{0}}{C}
, then take
l>2[\frac{2}{c_{0}}(\ln\frac{C}{\nu_{0}}+\ln\frac{1}{\delta})]^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}
such that
2Ce^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}<2\mu_{0}\delta
. One thereby has

\displaystyle 5\mu_{0}\delta-C\delta\hbar-C\delta\hbar^{\frac{1}{2}}-C(\delta+\hbar^{\frac{1}{2}}+1)e^{-\frac{c_{0}}{2}(l/2)^{\frac{2}{3-\gamma}}}\geq\mu_{0}\delta\,,\quad C(\delta\hbar+\hbar+\hbar^{\frac{1}{2}})\leq 2C\hbar^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

Therefore, the results in Lemma
2.6
hold and the proof is finished.
∎

9.2.
Weighted
L^{\infty}_{x,v}
estimate of
\mathbf{D}

We now study the weighted
L^{\infty}_{x,v}
estimate of the artificial damping operator
\mathbf{D}
, hence, to verify Lemma
2.7
.

Proof of Lemma
2.7
.

For the simplicity of notations, we employ the notation
\mathfrak{p}(x,v)
given in (
8.4
), i.e.,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{p}(x,v)=\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)}w_{\beta,\vartheta}(v)\,.

Then one has

\displaystyle\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}e^{\hbar\sigma}\mathbf{D}g\|_{\beta,\vartheta}=\|\mathfrak{p}\mathbf{D}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\,,\quad\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}e^{\hbar\sigma}g\|_{\beta-\tilde{N},\vartheta}=\|(1+|v|)^{-\tilde{N}}\mathfrak{p}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\,.

Now we control the norm
\|\mathfrak{p}\mathbf{D}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
. Recalling the definition of
\mathbf{D}g
in (
1.63
), one has

\displaystyle\mathfrak{p}\mathbf{D}g(x,v)=\sum_{j=0}^{3}\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathfrak{d}_{j}(x,v,\tilde{v})(1+\tilde{v})^{-\tilde{N}}\mathfrak{p}(x,\tilde{v})g(x,\tilde{v})\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}}_{\mathbf{P}_{j}}\,,

where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}_{0}(x,v,\tilde{v})=(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widehat{\mathbb{B}}_{3}\mathbb{B}_{3}\mathrm{d}v)^{-1}\ss_{0}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\tfrac{\mathfrak{p}(x,v)}{\mathfrak{p}(x,\tilde{v})}\tilde{v}_{3}(1+|\tilde{v}|)^{\tilde{N}}\widehat{\mathbb{B}}_{3}(\tilde{v})\psi_{0}^{*}(v)\,,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}_{j}(x,v,\tilde{v})=(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{j3}\mathbb{A}_{j3}\mathrm{d}v)^{-1}\ss_{0}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\tfrac{\mathfrak{p}(x,v)}{\mathfrak{p}(x,\tilde{v})}\tilde{v}_{3}(1+|\tilde{v}|)^{\tilde{N}}\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{j3}(\tilde{v})\psi_{j}^{*}(v)\,,\ j=1,2\,,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}_{3}(x,v,\tilde{v})=\ss_{+}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\tfrac{\mathfrak{p}(x,v)}{\mathfrak{p}(x,\tilde{v})}\tilde{v}_{3}(1+|\tilde{v}|)^{\tilde{N}}\psi_{3}^{*}(\tilde{v})\psi_{3}^{*}(v)\,.

We first control the quantity
\mathbf{P}_{0}
. More precisely, it holds

\displaystyle|\mathbf{P}_{0}|\leq\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}e^{\hbar\sigma}g\|_{\beta-\tilde{N},\vartheta}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathfrak{d}_{0}(x,v,\tilde{v})|\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}

By (
8.12
), it infers

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathfrak{p}(x,v)}{\mathfrak{p}(x,\tilde{v})}\leq C(1+|v|)^{k}(1+|\tilde{v}|)^{k}\mathrm{exp}\big[(c\hbar+\vartheta)(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|\tilde{v}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})\big]\,.

Moreover, it is easy to see that

\displaystyle|\widehat{\mathbb{B}}_{3}(\tilde{v})\psi_{0}^{*}(v)|\leq C\mathrm{exp}\big[-c^{\prime}(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|\tilde{v}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})\big]\,.

Note that

\displaystyle|\tilde{v}_{3}|(1+|\tilde{v}|)^{k+\tilde{N}}(1+|v|)^{k}\mathrm{exp}\big[-\tfrac{c^{\prime}}{2}(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|\tilde{v}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})\big]\leq C\,.

It therefore follows that

(9.30)

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{d}_{0}(x,v,\tilde{v})|\leq C\ss_{0}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\mathrm{exp}\big[-s_{0}(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|\tilde{v}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})\big]\,,

where
s_{0}=\tfrac{c^{\prime}}{2}-c\hbar-\vartheta>0
provived that
\hbar,\vartheta\geq 0
is sufficiently small. Consequently, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathfrak{d}_{j}(x,v,\tilde{v})|\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\leq C\tau(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{exp}\big[-s_{0}(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|\tilde{v}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})\big]\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\leq C\tau

uniformly in
(x,v)\in[0,+\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, which concludes that

(9.31)

\displaystyle|\mathbf{P}_{0}|\leq C\ss_{0}\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}e^{\hbar\sigma}g\|_{\beta-\tilde{N},\vartheta}\leq C\delta\hbar\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}e^{\hbar\sigma}g\|_{\beta-\tilde{N},\vartheta}\,.

Following the almost same arguments in (
9.31
), one has

(9.32)

\displaystyle|\mathbf{P}_{j}|\leq C\delta\hbar\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}e^{\hbar\sigma}g\|_{\beta-\tilde{N},\vartheta}

for
j=1,2,3
. As a result, the bounds (
9.31
) and (
9.32
) conclude the estimate (
2.15
), and the proof of Lemma
2.7
is completed.
∎

9.3.
Boundedness of
\mathbf{D}
from weighted
L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}
to
L^{\infty}_{x,v}

We now study the boundedness of
\mathbf{D}
from weighted
L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}
to
L^{\infty}_{x,v}
, hence, to verify Lemma
2.8
.

Proof of Lemma
2.8
.

Note that

\displaystyle[\![\nu^{-1}\mathbf{D}_{\hbar}g]\!]_{A;m,0,\vartheta}\leq C\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)\mathbf{D}_{\hbar}g(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\,,

and the definition of
\mathbf{D}_{h}
in (
2.13
) reads

(9.33)

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\hbar}g(x,v)=

\displaystyle\ss_{0}\kappa_{0}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,\tilde{v})}g(x,\tilde{v})\psi_{0}^{*}(v)\tilde{v}_{3}\widehat{\mathbb{B}}_{3}(\tilde{v})\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}

\displaystyle+\ss_{0}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\sum_{j=1}^{2}\kappa_{j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,\tilde{v})}g(x,\tilde{v})\psi_{j}^{*}(v)\tilde{v}_{3}\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{j3}(\tilde{v})\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}

\displaystyle+\ss_{+}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,\tilde{v})}g(x,\tilde{v})\psi_{3}^{*}(v)\tilde{v}_{3}\psi_{3}^{*}(\tilde{v})\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\,,

where the constants
\kappa_{0}=(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widehat{\mathbb{B}}_{3}\mathbb{B}_{3}\mathrm{d}v)^{-1}
and
\kappa_{j}=(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{j3}\mathbb{A}_{j3}\mathrm{d}v)^{-1}
with
j=1,2
. It thereby follows that

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)\mathbf{D}_{\hbar}g(x,v)|\leq\sum_{j=0}^{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha^{\prime}}(\tilde{v})\mathfrak{b}_{j}(x,v,\tilde{v})\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,\tilde{v})|z_{\alpha^{\prime}}(\tilde{v})g(x,\tilde{v})|\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\,,

where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{b}_{0}(x,v,\tilde{v})=

\displaystyle\ss_{0}\kappa_{0}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)\sigma_{x}^{-\frac{m}{2}}(x,\tilde{v})e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,\tilde{v})}|w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)\psi_{0}^{*}(v)\tilde{v}_{3}\widehat{\mathbb{B}}_{3}(\tilde{v})|\,,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{b}_{j}(x,v,\tilde{v})=

\displaystyle\ss_{0}\kappa_{j}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)\sigma_{x}^{-\frac{m}{2}}(x,\tilde{v})e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,\tilde{v})}|w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)\psi_{j}^{*}(v)\tilde{v}_{3}\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{j3}(\tilde{v})|\,,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{b}_{3}(x,v,\tilde{v})=

\displaystyle\ss_{+}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)\sigma_{x}^{-\frac{m}{2}}(x,\tilde{v})e^{\hbar\sigma(x,v)-\hbar\sigma(x,\tilde{v})}|w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)\psi_{3}^{*}(v)\tilde{v}_{3}\psi_{3}^{*}(\tilde{v})|\,,

for
j=1,2
. Together with Lemma
2.1
, (
8.11
), and the facts of exponential decay behavior of the functions
\psi_{j}^{*}(v)(j=0,1,2,3)
and
\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{j3}(v)(j=1,2)
,
\widehat{\mathbb{B}}_{3}(v)
, one gains

(9.34)

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{3}\mathfrak{b}_{j}(x,v,\tilde{v})\leq Ce^{-\frac{c^{\prime}}{2}(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|\tilde{v}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})}

for small enough
\hbar,\vartheta\geq 0
. Then

\displaystyle|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,v)w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}(v)\mathbf{D}_{\hbar}g(x,v)|\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha^{\prime}}(\tilde{v})e^{-\frac{c^{\prime}}{2}(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|\tilde{v}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}(x,\tilde{v})|z_{\alpha^{\prime}}(\tilde{v})g(x,\tilde{v})|\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha^{\prime}}^{2}(\tilde{v})e^{-c^{\prime}(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|\tilde{v}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})}\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}z_{\alpha^{\prime}}g(x,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}z_{\alpha^{\prime}}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\leq C\|\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}z_{\alpha^{\prime}}w_{-\gamma,\vartheta}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\,.

Here
0\leq\alpha^{\prime}<\frac{1}{2}
is required. Then the proof of Lemma
2.8
is completed.
∎

9.4.
Weighted
L^{2}_{v}
boundedness of
\mathbf{D}

We now study the weighted
L^{2}_{v}
boundedness of
\mathbf{D}
, hence, to verify Lemma
2.9
.

Proof of Lemma
2.9
.

Recalling (
9.33
), one easily has

\displaystyle\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}\mathbf{D}_{\hbar}g(x,v)=\sum_{j=0}^{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathfrak{n}_{j}(x,v,\tilde{v})\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,\tilde{v})\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\,,

where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{n}_{0}(x,v,\tilde{v})=

\displaystyle\ss_{0}\kappa_{0}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\tfrac{\mathfrak{p}(x,v)}{\mathfrak{p}(x,\tilde{v})}z_{-\alpha}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\tilde{v})|\psi_{0}^{*}(v)\tilde{v}_{3}\widehat{\mathbb{B}}_{3}(\tilde{v})|\,,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{n}_{j}(x,v,\tilde{v})=

\displaystyle\ss_{0}\kappa_{j}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\tfrac{\mathfrak{p}(x,v)}{\mathfrak{p}(x,\tilde{v})}z_{-\alpha}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\tilde{v})|\psi_{j}^{*}(v)\tilde{v}_{3}\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{j3}(\tilde{v})|\,,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{n}_{3}(x,v,\tilde{v})=

\displaystyle\ss_{+}(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\tfrac{\mathfrak{p}(x,v)}{\mathfrak{p}(x,\tilde{v})}z_{-\alpha}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\tilde{v})|\psi_{3}^{*}(v)\tilde{v}_{3}\psi_{3}^{*}(\tilde{v})|\,,

for
j=1,2
. Here the function
\mathfrak{p}(x,v)
is defined in (
8.4
). Following the similar arguments in (
9.34
), and combining with the fact
(\delta x+l)^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3-\gamma}}\lesssim 1
uniformly in
x\geq 0
, one infers that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{3}|\mathfrak{n}_{j}(x,v,\tilde{v})|\lesssim z_{-\alpha}(v)e^{-\frac{c^{\prime}}{2}(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|\tilde{v}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})}

uniformly in
x\geq 0
. Thanks to
0\leq\alpha<\frac{1}{2}
, it holds

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{3}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathfrak{n}_{j}(x,v,\tilde{v})|^{2}\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}z_{-\alpha}^{2}(v)e^{-c^{\prime}(|v-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+|\tilde{v}-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})}\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\mathrm{d}v\lesssim 1

uniformly in
x\geq 0
. Consequently, together with the Hölder inequality, one has

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}z_{-\alpha}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{3}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}\mathbf{D}_{\hbar}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big(\sum_{j=0}^{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathfrak{n}_{j}(x,v,\tilde{v})\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,\tilde{v})\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\Big)^{2}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\sum_{j=0}^{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathfrak{n}_{j}(x,v,\tilde{v})|^{2}\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\big)\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,\tilde{v})|^{2}\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\big)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{3}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathfrak{n}_{j}(x,v,\tilde{v})|^{2}\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}\mathrm{d}v\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma_{x}^{\frac{m}{2}}w_{\beta,\vartheta}g(x,v)|^{2}\mathrm{d}v\,.

The proof of Lemma
2.9
is therefore finished.
∎
